第二次世界大战以来，史学家们更关注从强权关系的角度考察美国的外交政策。Robert Osgood, Ideals and Self-Interest in America’s Foreign Relations (1953) 是这一学派最好的著作。Edward Buehrig, Woodrow Wilson and the Balance of Power (1955) 同样着重于强权方面的考量。比里格还编有 Wilson’s Foreign Policy in Perspective (1957)。他在 “Wilson’s Neutrality Re-Examined,” World Politics, III (1950) 中更简要地阐述了自己的观点。关于美国参战，最持平的研究之一是 Arthur Link, Wilson the Diplomatist (1957)；林克大体上沿袭西摩，但根据后来的研究成果对西摩作了修正。

David M. Kennedy, Over Here (1980) 是关于第一次世界大战对美国社会影响的最佳著作。关于工业动员，参见 Paul A. C. Koistinen, “The ‘Industrial-Military Complex’ in Historical Perspective: World War I,” Business History Review, 41 (1967)；Robert D. Cuff, The War Industries Board (1973)；Daniel R. Beaver, Newton D. Baker and the American War Effort, 1917–1919 (1966)；Valerie Jean Conner, The National War Labor Board (1983)；Maurine W. Greenwald, Women, War, and Work (1980)；James P. Johnson, The Politics of Soft Coal (1979)；Harold M. Hyman, Soldiers and Spruce (1963)；William J. Breen, Uncle Sam at Home (1984)；以及 Ronald Schaffer, America in the Great War (1991)。关于战时工业委员会的首脑伯纳德·巴鲁克，Jordan Schwarz, The Speculator (1981) 是一流之作。Seward W. Livermore, Politics is Adjourned (1966) 论述了战时的国会。George T. Blakely, Historians on the Homefront (1970) 记述学者们向国家屈膝，读来令人不安；John A. Thompson, American Progressive Publicists and the First World War (1987) 则是一部鞭辟入里的分析。对学术界而言尤为重要的是 Carol S. Gruber, Mars and Minerva (1976)。关于“战时福利国家”的成长，可比较 Allen F. Davis, “Welfare Reform and World War I,” American Quarterly, 19 (1967) 和 Christopher Lasch, The New Radicalism in America (1965) 中两种截然不同的观点。Allan M. Brandt, No Magic Bullet (1985) 对战时卖淫问题的论述十分出色。Robert H. Ferrell, Woodrow Wilson and World War I, 1917–1921 (1985) 是标准的综述。

一些较早的研究仍然值得参考。Frederick Paxson, American Democracy and the World War (3 vols., 1936–48) 长期以来是标准资料，其补充有 Benedict Crowell and R. F. Wilson (eds.), How America Went to War (6 vols., 1921)；Grosvenor Clarkson, Industrial America in the World War (1923)；以及 Bernard Baruch, American Industry in War (1941)。George Mowry, “The First World War and American Democracy,” 载于 Jesse Clarkson and Thomas Cochran (eds.), War as a Social Institution (1941)，讨论了战争的恶劣影响；Max Lerner 在对莫里这篇文章的评论中提出了异议。Sidney Kaplan, “Social Engineers as Saviors: Effects of World War I on Some American Liberals,” Journal of the History of Ideas, XVII (1956) 颇具启发性。William Waller (ed.), War in the Twentieth Century (1940) 汇集了若干关于第一次世界大战及其后果的文章。

关于陆军和海军方面的战事，最可靠的资料是 Harvey A. DeWeerd, President Wilson Fights His War (1968)；Edward M. Coffman, The War to End Wars (1968)；Russell F. Weigley, The American Way of War (1973)，其中包括对美国远征军的分析；以及 Frank Freidel, Over There (1990)。John Whiteclay Chambers II, To Raise an Army (1987) 远远超出了征兵这一主题，阐明了进步主义与民族主义之间的关系。关于黑人部队的经历，参见 Arthur E. Barbeau and Florette Henri, The Unknown Soldiers (1974)。仍有价值的较早著作包括 Elting Morison, Admiral Sims and the Modern American Navy (1942)；J. G. Harbord, The American Army in France, 1917–1918 (1936)；John J. Pershing, Final Report (1919)；Leonard Ayres, The War with Germany (1919)；以及 George Davis, A Navy Second to None (1940)。George Sylvester Viereck (ed.), As They Saw Us (1929) 是一本引人入胜的书，福煦、鲁登道夫等人在书中谈了他们对在法美军的印象；鲁登道夫的那篇文章是他那套怪异政治观点在希特勒上台之前的一个样本。

关于对公民自由的压制，参见 Harry N. Scheiber, The Wilson Administration and Civil Liberties, 1917–1921 (1960)；Donald Johnson, The Challenge to American Freedoms (1963)；H. C. Peterson and Gilbert Fite, Opponents of War (1957)；Joan M. Jensen, The Price of Vigilance (1968)；Frederick C. Luebke, Bonds of Loyalty (1974)；以及 Charles Stewart, “Prussianizing Wisconsin,” Atlantic Monthly, CXXIII (1919)。Stephen L. Vaughn, Holding Fast to Inner Lines (1980) 对克里尔委员会作了新的审视。Carl Resek, War and the Intellectuals (1964) 是关于那位富于挑战性的随笔作家伦道夫·伯恩的最佳资料。Richard Polenberg, Fighting Faiths (1987) 对艾布拉姆斯案的论述十分出色。

在过去三十余年里，一批才华横溢的史学家使我们对凡尔赛会议的利害所在，以及围绕美国是否应当加入国际联盟的斗争，有了新的理解。尤其参见 David F. Trask, The United States in the Supreme War Council (1961)；Lawrence E. Gelfand, The Inquiry (1963)；N. Gordon Levin, Jr., Woodrow Wilson and World Politics (1968)；Ralph A. Stone, The Irreconcilables (1970)；Arthur Walworth, Wilson and His Peacemakers (1986)；Lloyd Ambrosius, Woodrow Wilson and the American Diplomatic Tradition (1988)；以及 Arno J. Mayer 的两本书：Wilson vs. Lenin (1959) 和 Politics and Diplomacy of Peacemaking (1967)。Christopher Lasch, The American Liberals and the Russian Revolution (1962) 是一部文笔上佳的记述。

对于关于凡尔赛会议的早期文献，最好的入门是 R. C. Binkley, “Ten Years of Peace Conference History,” Journal of Modern History, I (1929)，以及 Paul Birdsall, “The Second Decade of Peace Conference History,” Journal of Modern History, XI (1939)。H. W. V. Temperley et al., A History of the Peace Conference of Paris (6 vols., 1920–24) 是标准资料，但 Thomas Bailey, Woodrow Wilson and the Lost Peace (1944) 更有收获。关于威尔逊的作用，参见 R. S. Baker, Woodrow Wilson and World Settlement (3 vols., 1922)。

John Maynard Keynes, The Economic Consequences of the Peace (1920) 对人们关于这次会议的看法产生了巨大影响；凯恩斯妙语连珠的机智掩盖了其思想的薄弱。Étienne Mantoux, The Carthaginian Peace, or The Economic Consequences of Mr. Keynes (1946) 作了雄辩的回应。Paul Birdsall, Versailles Twenty Years After (1941) 是一部重要的重新评价。Thomas Bailey, Woodrow Wilson and the Great Betrayal (1945) 是关于国联之争的最佳著作，尽管贝利对威尔逊过于苛刻。John Garraty, Henry Cabot Lodge (1953) 比早先的记述对传主更为友善，但洛奇的可憎还是压倒了加拉蒂的宽厚。Dexter Perkins, “Woodrow Wilson’s Tour,” 载于 Daniel Aaron (ed.), America in Crisis (1952)，对那段悲剧性的插曲作了令人钦佩的论述。

Robert Murray, Red Scare (1955) 是关于战后歇斯底里的标准著作，但学生们还应参考 Stanley Coben, A. Mitchell Palmer (1963)；William Preston, Jr., Aliens and Dissenters (1963)；以及 Paul L. Murphy, The Meaning of Freedom of Speech (1972)。John Blum, “Nativism, Anti-Radicalism, and the Foreign Scare, 1917–1920,” Midwest Journal, III (1950–51) 鞭辟入里，Stanley Coben 的 “A Study in Nativism: The American Red Scare of 1919–1920,” Political Science Quarterly, LXXIX (1964) 则堪称精彩。红色恐慌的气息保存在 Calvin Coolidge, “Enemies of the Republic: Are the ‘Reds’ Stalking Our College Women?” Delineator, XCVIII (1921) 之中。R. L. Friedheim, The Seattle General Strike (1965)；Kate Holladay Claghorn, The Immigrant’s Day in Court (1923)，其中有一章关于驱逐出境的内容很好；以及 Nelson Van Valen, “The Bolsheviki and the Orange Growers,” Pacific Historical Review, XXII (1953)，都是专题研究。Francis Russell, “Coolidge and the Boston Police Strike,” Antioch Review, XVI (1956) 提供了一段愉快的回忆。另见他的 A City in Terror (1975)。Zechariah Chafee, Free Speech in the United States (rev. ed., 1941) 是关于这一时期侵犯公民自由的经典记述。

Theodore Draper, The Roots of American Communism (1957) 为他的共产党史开了一个出色的头。Joseph Freeman, An American Testament (1936) 也许是美国马克思主义者写出的最好的一本书。研究美国社会主义的基本资料是 David Shannon, The Socialist Party of America (1955)；Daniel Bell, “Marxian Socialism in the United States,” 载于 Donald Egbert and Stow Persons, Socialism and American Life (1952), Vol. I；Ray Ginger, The Bending Cross (1949)，一部德布斯传记；以及 James Weinstein, The Decline of Socialism in America, 1912–1925 (1967)。William L. O’Neill, A Better World (1982) 批评了知识分子对斯大林主义的迷恋。Granville Hicks, John Reed (1936) 写于希克斯还是马克思主义者的时候，颇为重要。G. L. Joughin and E. M. Morgan, The Legacy of Sacco and Vanzetti (1948) 不可或缺，但也请参看 David Felix, Protest (1965)。Francis Russell 在 Tragedy in Dedham (1962) 和 Sacco and Vanzetti (1986) 中对萨科是否无辜提出了质疑。

Arthur Schlesinger, Jr., The Crisis of the Old Order, 1919–1933 (1957) 是一位自由派党人对这一时期政治史文笔上佳的导论。关于威尔逊时代的终结，参见 Gene Smith, When the Cheering Stopped (1964)；Melvin I. Urofsky, Big Steel and the Wilson Administration (1969)；以及 Burl Noggle, Into the Twenties (1974)。关于这一时期最好的政治著述有许多出自新闻记者之手。William Allen White, Autobiography (1946)；他的 Masks in a Pageant (1930)；Walter Johnson (ed.), Selected Letters of William Allen White, 1899–1943 (1947)；以及 Johnson, William Allen White’s America (1947)，都使史学家受惠于这位“恩波里亚的贤人”。Clinton Gilbert 匿名写了 The Mirrors of Washington (1921)，又署名写了 “You Takes Your Choice” (1924)。关于共和党政治人物的较重要资料有 Henry Pringle, The Life and Times of William Howard Taft (1939), Vol. II；William Hutchinson, Lowden of Illinois (2 vols., 1957)；以及 Joel Paschal, Mr. Justice Sutherland (1951)。

Robert K. Murray, The Harding Era (1969) 是关于这一题目最厚实的资料，但也请参看 Andrew Sinclair, The Available Man (1965)；Eugene P. Trani and David L. Wilson, The Presidency of Warren G. Harding (1977)；关于1920年大选，则有 Wesley Bagby, The Road to Normalcy (1962)。关于哈定时代及其后的各项政策，可查阅 Ellis W. Hawley, ed., Herbert Hoover as Secretary of Commerce (1981)；Craig Lloyd, Aggressive Introvert (1972)；Donald L. Winters, Henry Cantwell Wallace as Secretary of Agriculture, 1921–1924 (1970)；以及 Donald C. Swain, Federal Conservation Policy, 1921–1933 (1963)。关于这一时期的大丑闻，最可靠的资料是 J. Leonard Bates, The Origins of Teapot Dome (1963) 和 Burl Noggle, Teapot Dome (1962)。Harry Daugherty and Thomas Dixon, The Inside Story of the Harding Tragedy (1932) 读时须持保留态度。

Donald R. McCoy, Calvin Coolidge (1967) 对这位“沉默的总统”作了并非毫无批评的评价，但谁也不该忽视 William Allen White, A Puritan in Babylon: The Story of Calvin Coolidge (1938)，这是我们的文献中最出色的政治传记之一。Claude Fuess, Calvin Coolidge (1940) 更为正统。Gamaliel Bradford, “The Genius of the Average: Calvin Coolidge,” Atlantic Monthly, CXLV (1930) 颇有见地。William H. Harbaugh, Lawyer’s Lawyer (1973) 是约翰·W·戴维斯的传记，是有史以来关于落选者的最佳研究之一；Robert K. Murray, The 103rd Ballot (1976) 读来令人愉快。

L. Ethan Ellis, Republican Foreign Policy, 1921–1933 (1968) 是一部标准的导论，但应以后来的著作加以补充，包括 Melvyn P. Leffler, The Elusive Quest (1979) 和 Warren Cohen, Empire without Tears (1987)。Jon Jacobson, “Is There a New International History of the 1920s?” American Historical Review, 88 (1983) 是一篇有价值的综述文章。Frank Simonds, American Foreign Policy in the Post-War Years (1935) 是一位集体安全反对者发人深省的分析；尽管其中心论点站不住脚，他却提出了不少精明的观察，尤其是在经济政策方面。Dexter Perkins, “The Department of State and American Public Opinion,” 载于 Gordon Craig and Felix Gilbert (eds.), The Diplomats 1919–1939 (1953)，十分出色。George Kennan, American Diplomacy, 1900–1950 (1952) 提出了如今已为人熟知的论点：20世纪的美国外交政策建立在关于道德与强权的不合理假设之上。

Selig Adler, “The War-Guilt Question and American Disillusionment, 1919–1928,” Journal of Modern History, XXIII (1951)，以及 “Isolationism Since 1914,” American Scholar, XXI (1952)，都追溯了美国退出世界事务的过程。值得注意的几种特别的解释是 William Appleman Williams, “The Legend of Isolationism in the 1920s,” Science and Society, XVIII (1954)；Daniel Boorstin, “America and the Image of Europe,” Perspectives USA, XIV (1956)；以及 Charles Beard, “The American Invasion of Europe,” Harper’s, CLVIII (1929)。Akira Iriye, After Imperialism (1965) 论述了远东事务，Joseph Tulchin, The Aftermath of War (1971) 论述了美国的拉丁美洲政策，后者也是 Robert F. Smith, The United States and Revolutionary Nationalism in Mexico, 1916–32 (1972) 的主题。

史学家们日益关注这些年间美国帝国主义的延续。Frank Costigliola, Awkward Dominion (1984) 和 Emily S. Rosenberg, Spreading the American Dream (1982) 都表明，在这个“孤立主义”的时代，美国仍在继续谋求海外利益。其他侧重经济的著作包括 Herbert Feis, The Diplomacy of the Dollar (1950)；Joseph Brandes, Herbert Hoover and Economic Diplomacy (1962)；Joan Hoff Wilson, American Business and Foreign Policy (1971)；以及 Michael J. Hogan, Informal Entente (1977)。Simon Kuznets, Economic Change (1953) 有一章关于对外经济关系的重要论述。F. W. Taussig, The Tariff History of the United States (1923) 有一章专论1922年关税法，作者是贸易保护主义最主要的学术批评者。Abraham Berglund, “The Tariff Act of 1922,” American Economic Review, XIII (1923) 对《福德尼-麦坎伯关税法》作了另一番有价值的评述。关于对外经济政策的早期著作中，最好的是 James Angell, Financial Foreign Policy of the United States (1933)；M. F. Jolliffe, The United States as a Financial Centre 1919–1933 (1935)；Benjamin Williams, Economic Foreign Policy of the United States (1929)；以及 John Madden, Marcus Nadler, and Harry Sauvain, America’s Experience as a Creditor Nation (1937)。

Merlo Pusey, Charles Evans Hughes (1951), Vol. II 是关于这一时代最重要的国务卿的标准传记，但缺乏批评。阅读时应结合 Dexter Perkins 的小册子 Charles Evans Hughes and American Democratic Statesmanship (1956)。Thomas H. Buckley, The United States and the Washington Conference (1970) 是关于该题目最新的著作，但也应参考 John Chalmers Vinson, The Parchment Peace (1955)；A. Whitney Griswold, The Far Eastern Policy of the United States (1943)；以及 H. H. and Margaret Sprout, Toward a New Order of Sea Power (1940)。Robert Ferrell, Peace in Their Time (1952) 对《凯洛格-白里安公约》的论述很好；另见 Waldo Chamberlin, “Origins of the Kellogg-Briand Pact,” Historian, XV (1952)。

W. S. Myers, The Foreign Policies of Herbert Hoover, 1929–1933 (1940) 是标准著作。E. E. Schattschneider, Politics, Pressures, and the Tariff (1935) 和 J. M. Jones, Tariff Retaliation (1934) 讨论了《斯穆特-霍利关税法》的通过及其影响。Henry Stimson and M. Bundy, On Active Service in Peace and War (1948) 是史汀生的自述，而 Richard Current, Secretary Stimson (1954) 则把胡佛的这位国务卿狠狠批评了一通。Elting E. Morison, Turmoil and Tradition (1960) 态度更为友善。关于史汀生与远东，参见 Robert Langer, Seizure of Territory (1947) 和 Sara Smith, The Manchurian Crisis, 1931–1932 (1948)。Robert Ferrell, American Diplomacy in the Great Depression (1957) 是对胡佛外交政策最系统的分析。

关于进步主义在20年代的命运，可见 Richard Hofstadter, The Age of Reform (1955)；Eric Goldman, Rendezvous with Destiny (1952)；Arthur Ekirch, The Decline of American Liberalism (1955)；以及 Peter Filene, “An Obituary for the Progressive Movement,” American Quarterly, XXII (1968)。关于进步主义瓦解的重要地区性研究包括 C. Vann Woodward, Tom Watson (1938)，一部关于这位佐治亚州平民党人的一流传记；George Mowry, The California Progressives (1951)；以及 Albert Kirwan, Revolt of the Rednecks (1951)。这一时期的回忆录中最令人着迷的是 The Autobiography of Lincoln Steffens (1931)，但 Robert Morss Lovett, All Our Years (1948) 也不应忽视。Edgar Kemler, The Deflation of American Ideals (1941) 颇具反传统色彩。Claude Bowers, Beveridge and the Progressive Era (1932) 强调民族主义在进步主义运动中的作用。关于主要进步派人物的传记，内容翔实的有 Howard Zinn, La Guardia in Congress (1958)；Arthur Mann, La Guardia (1959)；以及 Richard M. Lowitt, George Norris (1963)。Preston Hubbard, Jr. 贡献了 Origins of the T. V. A. (1961)。

Charles Forcey, The Crossroads of Liberalism (1961) 深思熟虑地分析了赫伯特·克罗利、沃尔特·韦尔和沃尔特·李普曼从1900年到1925年的思想。Ronald Steel, Walter Lippmann and the American Century (1980) 是一部力作。David Noble, “The New Republic and the Idea of Progress, 1914–1920,” Mississippi Valley Historical Review, XXXVIII (1951) 追溯了进步派知识分子一翼信心的丧失。关于当时人对进步主义的分析，参见 Walter Weyl, Tired Radicals and Other Essays (1919)；Herbert Croly, “The Eclipse of Progressivism,” New Republic, XXIV (October 27, 1920)，以及 “The Outlook for Progressivism in Politics,” New Republic, XLI (December 10, 1924)；还有 Charles Merz, “Progressivism, Old and New,” Atlantic Monthly, CXXXII (1923)。

Kenneth MacKay, The Progressive Movement of 1924 (1947) 是标准的专著。1924年反叛的背景在 F. E. Haynes, Social Politics in the United States (1924) 和 Robert Morlan, Political Prairie Fire: The Nonpartisan League, 1915–1922 (1955) 中有所阐述。Alexander Harvey, “The Advantage of Senator La Follette,” American Mercury, III (1924) 对“斗士鲍勃”作了精明的分析。主要的传记是 Belle Case and Fola La Follette, Robert M. La Follette (2 vols., 1953)，毫不掩饰地崇拜传主，但仍然重要。选举结果在 Hugh Keenleyside, “The American Political Revolution of 1924,” Current History, XXI (1925) 和 James Shideler, “The Disintegration of the Progressive Party Movement of 1924,” Historian (1951) 中得到了考察。Paul Carter, The Decline and Revival of the Social Gospel (1956) 是关于1920年至1940年美国新教的一部出色研究。Clarke Chambers 在 Seedtime of Reform (1963) 中论证了进步主义在20年代的延续，这一主题最早是在 Arthur S. Link, “What Happened to the Progressive Movement?” American Historical Review, LXIV (1959) 中提出的。与进步主义相关的知识分子研究中，重要的有 Richard Hofstadter, The Progressive Historians (1968)；David W. Levy, Herbert Croly of The New Republic (1985)；John P. Diggins, The Bard of Savagery (1978)，论托斯丹·凡勃伦；以及 Steven C. Rockefeller, John Dewey (1991)，该书强调这位哲学家的宗教观。

Gilbert Fite, George Peek and the Fight for Farm Parity (1954)，是论述20世纪20年代农业政治的最佳著作。其他主要资料有 Theodore Saloutos and John Hicks, Agricultural Discontent in the Middle West, 1900–1939 (1951)；Grant McConnell, Decline of Agrarian Democracy (1953)；Harold Barger and Hans Landsberg, American Agriculture, 1899–1939 (1942)；Russell Lord, The Wallaces of Iowa (1947)；以及 James H. Shideler, Farm Crisis, 1919–1923 (1957)。较有启发性的文章有两篇：Malcolm Sillars, “Henry A. Wallace’s Editorials on Agricultural Discontent, 1921–1928,” Agricultural History, XXVI (1952)，以及 Alice Christensen, “Agricultural Pressure and Governmental Response, 1919–1929,” Agricultural History, XI (1937)。

关于这一时期的劳工史，Irving Bernstein, The Lean Years (1960) 不可或缺。其他有用的资料包括 David Brody, The Steel Strike of 1919 (1965)；Robert Zieger, Republicans and Labor, 1919–1929 (1969)；Selig Perlman and Philip Taft, History of Labor in the United States, 1896–1932，此书是 John Commons 鸿篇巨制“History of Labour in the United States”（4 vols., 1918–35）的最后一卷；以及 Leo Wolman, Growth of American Trade Unions, 1880–1923 (1924)。关于世界产业工人联合会，Melvin Dubofsky, We Shall Be All (1969) 取代了 Paul Brissenden, The I. W.W. (1919)、J. S. Gambs, The Decline of the I. W.W. (1932) 和 Ralph Chaplin, Wobbly (1948)。Philip Taft, The A.F. of L. in the Time of Gompers (1957) 对美国劳工联合会持同情态度。J. B. S. Hardman (ed.), American Labor Dynamics (1928) 对1918年至1928年的劳工运动作了一番尸检式的剖析。较重要的文章包括 Sumner Slichter, “The Current Labor Policies of American Industries,” Quarterly Journal of Economics, XLIII (1929)；David Saposs, “The American Labor Movement Since the War,” Quarterly Journal of Economics, XLIX (1935)；以及 Lyle Cooper, “The American Labor Movement in Prosperity and Depression,” American Economic Review, XXII (1932)。关于服装业工人，Matthew Josephson, Sidney Hillman (1952) 必不可少。

关于这一时代的思想史与文化史，文献极为丰富。最好的指南是 Henry F. May, The End of American Innocence (1959)；Loren Baritz, The Culture of the Twenties (1969)；Alfred Kazin, On Native Grounds (1942)；Frederick Hoffman, The Twenties (1955) 和 The Modern Novel in America, 1900–1950 (1951)；Maxwell Geismar, Last of the Provincials (1947)；J. W. Beach, American Fiction, 1920–1940 (1941)；Malcolm Cowley (ed.), After the Genteel Tradition (1937)；Oscar Cargill, Intellectual America (1941)；以及 Stanley Cooperman, World War I and the American Novel (1967)。John Hutchens, The American Twenties (1952) 是这一时代文学作品的选集。Edmund Wilson 把他在这些年间所写的随笔和应时文字结集为两卷：The Shores of Light (1952) 和 The American Earthquake (1958)。Van Wyck Brooks, The Confident Years 1885–1915 (1952)，以略显倦怠的印象派笔法记述战前岁月，是他“Makers and Finders”系列的第五卷亦即最后一卷。他的 Days of the Phoenix (1957) 是自传。

Malcolm Cowley, Exile’s Return (1934; new ed., 1951) 是对“迷惘的一代”的一部引人遐思的综述。关于这一时期较有见地的文章有 Henry May, “The Rebellion of the Intellectuals, 1912–1917,” American Quarterly, VIII (1956)；W. H. Auden, “Henry James and the Artist in America,” Harper’s, CXCVII (1948)；John Aldridge, “The Predicament of Today’s Writer,” Sarah Lawrence Alumnae Magazine, XXII (1957)；T. R. Fyvel, “Martin Arrowsmith and His Habitat,” New Republic, CXXXIII (July 18, 1955)；以及 Arthur Mizener, “The Novel in America: 1920–1940,” Perspectives USA, XV (1956)。George Snyderman and William Josephs, “Bohemia: The Underworld of Art,” Social Forces, XVIII (1939) 是一篇朴实无华的社会分析。

这一时期阴郁的思想氛围，在 Joseph Wood Krutch, The Modern Temper (1929) 和 Walter Lippmann, A Preface to Morals (1929) 中得到了捕捉。Henry Harrison, “Last Days of the Devastators,” Yale Review, XVIII (1928) 是对当时那种自我鞭挞风气的一篇精明评论。Harold Stearns 的 America and the Young Intellectual (1921) 和 Civilization in the United States (1922) 是里程碑式的作品。Mabel Dodge Luhan, Intimate Memories (4 vols., 1933–37) 引人入胜。关于H·L·门肯，见 Edgar Kemler, The Irreverent Mr. Mencken (1950)；William Manchester, Disturber of the Peace (1951)；Charles Angoff, H. L. Mencken (1956)；以及 Fred C. Hobson, Serpent in Eden (1974)。

Morton White, Social Thought in America (1949) 是对“反形式主义的反叛”的一部深入研究。Howard Mumford Jones, The Bright Medusa (1952) 论证说，20世纪20年代青年以艺术之名发动的反叛不过是一个悠久传统的顶点，但论证并不令人信服。Bernard De Voto, Forays and Rebuttals (1936) 收有一篇随笔 “The Well-Informed, 1920–1930”，以及一篇对 Cowley 的 Exile’s Return 充满敌意的书评。De Voto 的 The Literary Fallacy (1944) 是对20世纪20年代作家们的严厉控诉，是一部见识与谬论奇特混杂的书。Stanley Edgar Hyman, The Armed Vision (1948) 中有一篇针对 Van Wyck Brooks 的杀伤力极强的文章。Lionel Trilling, The Liberal Imagination (1953) 是一部精彩的随笔集，其中有些篇什（如论 Sherwood Anderson 的那篇）直接论及20世纪20年代，而所有篇什都为认识这一时期提供了新鲜的洞见。

关于个别作家的较重要研究有 Mark Schorer, Sinclair Lewis (1961)；Carlos Baker, Ernest Hemingway (1969)；以及 Henry D. Piper, F. Scott Fitzgerald (1972)。Cleanth Brooks, William Faulkner (1963) 考察了约克纳帕塔法世界的创造者，Townsend Ludington 的 John Dos Passos (1980) 颇有见地。Elizabeth Drew, T. S. Eliot, The Design of His Poetry (1949) 清晰明了。在卷帙浩繁的艾略特研究文献中，F. O. Matthiessen, The Achievement of T. S. Eliot (2d ed., 1947)，以及 David Daiches, Poetry and the Modern World (1940)、Stephen Spender, The Destructive Element (1935) 和 John Crowe Ransom, The New Criticism (1941) 中的相关章节，值得注意。Hart Crane 先后是 Philip Horton（1937年）和 Brom Weber（1948年）研究的对象。Irving Howe, Sherwood Anderson (1951) 是一部简明而出色的评断。

关于这一时期的艺术，见 Barbara Rose, American Art Since 1900 (1967)；Sam Hunter, American Painting and Sculpture (1959)；Milton W. Brown, American Painting from the Armory Show to the Depression (1955)；Oliver Larkin, Art and Life in America (1949)；James Fitch, American Building (1948)；John Baur, Revolution and Tradition in Modern American Art (1951)；以及 Townsend Ludington, Marsden Hartley (1992)。Meyer Schapiro, “Rebellion in Art,” 载 Daniel Aaron (ed.), America in Crisis (1952)，对军械库展览的论述极为出色。

关于道德革命，Paula S. Fass, The Damned and the Beautiful (1977) 对20世纪20年代的青年颇有洞见。David M. Kennedy, Birth Control in America (1970) 探讨了玛格丽特·桑格的事业的意义。较晚近的记述是 Ellen Chesler, Woman of Valor (1992)。当时人的评论，见 Freda Kirchwey (ed.), Our Changing Morality: A Symposium (1924)；The Annals of the American Academy of Political and Social Science 1929年5月号和 Survey 1926年12月1日号，两者均为妇女专号；Katharine Fullerton Gerould, “Reflections of a Grundy Cousin,” Atlantic Monthly, CXXVI (1920)；John Carter, Jr., “‘These Wild Young People’: By One of Them,” Atlantic Monthly, CXXVI (1920)；Viola Paradise, “Sex Simplex,” Forum, LXXIV (1925)；Mary Agnes Hamilton, “‘Nothing Shocks Me,’” Harper’s, CLV (1927)；Dorothy Dunbar Bromley, “Feminist, New Style,” Harper’s, CLV (1927)；Eleanor Rowland Wembridge, “Petting and the Campus,” Survey, LIV (July 1, 1925)；William Bolitho, “The New Skirt Length,” Harper’s, CLX (1930)；以及 G. Stanley Hall, “Flapper Americana Novissima,” Atlantic Monthly, CXXIX (1922)。

Nathan G. Hale, Jr., Freud and the Americans (1971) 考察了1917年以前精神分析在美国的历史。Ernest Jones, The Life and Work of Sigmund Freud (3 vols., 1953–57) 是权威传记。Walter Lippmann, “Freud and the Layman,” New Republic, II (April 17, 1915) 是最早的赏识之作之一。George Santayana, “A Long Way Round to Nirvana: or, Much Ado About Dying,” Dial, LXXV (1923) 评论了弗洛伊德的 Beyond the Pleasure Principle。Joseph Jastrow, “The Freudian Temper: And Its Menace to the Lay Mind,” Century, CXIX (1929) 持批评态度。关于弗洛伊德在美国受到的接纳，见 A. A. Brill, “The Introduction and Development of Freud’s Work in the United States,” American Journal of Sociology, XLV (1939)；Celia Stendler, “New Ideas for Old: How Freudism Was Received in the United States from 1900 to 1925,” Journal of Educational Psychology, XXXVIII (1947)；Havelock Ellis, “Freud’s Influence on the Changed Attitude Toward Sex,” American Journal of Sociology, XLV (1939)；Karl Menninger, “Pseudoanalysis: Perils of Freudian Verbalisms,” Outlook, CLV (July 9, 1930)；以及 Grace Adams, “The Rise and Fall of Psychology,” Atlantic Monthly, CLIII (1934)。Frederick Hoffman, Freudianism and the Literary Mind (1945) 是关于这一题目的最佳资料。Maxwell Bodenheim, “Psychoanalysis and American Fiction,” The Nation, CXIV (June 7, 1922) 对美国文学中的“阳具夸张”不以为然，尤其看不上那些“年轻人仰面躺在玉米地里、为自己的身体而郁郁寡欢”的小说。Lucille Birnbaum, “Behaviorism in the 1920’s,” American Quarterly, VII (1955) 令人赞赏。

Dorothy M. Brown, Setting a Course (1987) 综述了20世纪20年代的美国妇女。William H. Chafe, The American Woman (1972) 是一部开创性的研究。William L. O’Neill, Everyone Was Brave (1969) 探讨了“女权主义的兴衰”，而 J. Stanley Lemon, The Woman Citizen (1973) 的评价则更为积极。其他重要著作包括 Aileen S. Kraditor, The Ideas of the Woman Suffrage Movement (1965)；Winifred Wandersee, Women’s Work and Family Values, 1920–1940 (1981)；Nancy Cott, The Grounding of Modern Feminism (1987)；以及 Estelle B. Freedman, “The New Woman: Changing Views of Women in the 1920s,” Journal of American History, LXI (1974)。

这一时代的黑人史，要从种族战争的可怕故事讲起：Arthur Waskow, From Race Riot to Sit-in (1966)；Elliot Rudwick, The Race Riot in East St. Louis, 1919 (1967)；以及论述芝加哥的 William Tuttle, Jr., Race Riot (1970)。Elliot Rudwick, W. E. B. Du Bois (1960) 论述的是这一时期堪称最重要的黑人人物，Nancy Weiss, The National Urban League (1974) 论述的则是一个重要组织。E. David Cronon, Black Moses (1955)、Theodore G. Vincent, Black Power and the Garvey Movement (1971)、Randall Burkett, Garveyism as a Religious Movement (1978) 和 Judith Stein, The World of Marcus Garvey (1986) 提供了不同的视角。关于内城区的成长，见 Allan H. Spear, Black Chicago (1967)；Gilbert Osofsky, Harlem: The Making of a Ghetto (1965)；Jervis Anderson, This Was Harlem (1981)；以及 David Levering Lewis, When Harlem Was in Vogue (1981)。Nathan Huggins, Harlem Renaissance (1971) 是一部细腻敏锐的研究。

有大量著述探讨这一时期的大众文化以及人们对当时英雄人物的反应。Kathy J. Ogren, The Jazz Revolution (1989) 是关于爵士乐这一大题目的最新著作。Robert W. Creamer, Babe (1974) 刻画了“强打之王”乔治·赫尔曼·鲁斯。Kenneth S. Davis, The Hero (1959) 是查尔斯·A·林德伯格的一幅肖像；林德伯格也是 John W. Ward, “The Meaning of Lindbergh’s Flight,” American Quarterly, 10 (1958) 的主题，那是一篇富于想象力和洞察力的杰出文章。

这一时代的经济已被研究得透彻至极。主要由于布鲁金斯学会和全国经济研究局的倡导，研究者面临的主要是一个如何取舍的问题。下面仅列出较重要的早期专著中的少数几种：Frederick Mills, Economic Tendencies in the United States (1932)；Arthur F. Burns, Production Trends in the United States since 1870 (1934)；Arthur R. Burns, The Decline of Competition (1936)；Solomon Fabricant, The Output of Manufacturing Industries, 1899–1937 (1940)；Jacob Gould, Output and Productivity in the Electric and Gas Utilities, 1899–1942 (1946)；Ralph Epstein, Industrial Profits in the United States (1934)；Charles Bliss, The Structure of Manufacturing Production (1946)；Harold Barger, Outlay and Income in the United States, 1921–1938 (1942)；Robert Gordon, Business Fluctuations (1952)；Joseph Schumpeter, Business Cycles (1939)；Edwin Nourse et al., America’s Capacity to Produce (1934)；Maurice Leven et al., America’s Capacity to Consume (1934)；George Stigler, Trends in Output and Employment (1947)；Thomas Wilson, Fluctuations in Income and Employment (1948)；Paul Douglas, Real Wages in the United States, 1890–1926 (1930)；以及 George Edwards, The Evolution of Finance Capitalism (1938)。Simon Kuznets 是这一时期伟大的创新者之一，著有 National Income and Its Composition, 1919–1938 (1941) 和 National Products since 1869 (1946)。President’s Conference on Unemployment, Recent Economic Changes in the United States (2 vols., 1929) 是一部极有价值的资料汇编。

最近几十年来，学者们既充实了这一蔚为可观的文献，也采取了新的研究路径。Alfred D. Chandler, Jr., Strategy and Structure (1962) 是一个重要的新起点，Louis Galambos, Competition and Cooperation (1966) 则指明了转向组织史取向的道路。其他重要专著包括 Loren Baritz, The Servants of Power (1960)；Elmus R. Wicker, Federal Reserve Monetary Policy, 1917–1933 (1966)；Morrell Heald, The Social Responsibilities of Business (1970)；Robert F. Himmelberg, The Origins of the National Recovery Administration (1976)；Stuart D. Brandes, American Welfare Capitalism, 1880–1940 (1976)；Roland Marchand, Advertising the American Dream (1985)；以及 Gary Alchon, The Invisible Hand of Planning (1985)。

George Soule, Prosperity Decade (1947) 是关于这一时期的最佳经济史。James Prothro, The Dollar Decade (1954) 以诙谐的笔调复述了工商界的思想。Charles Chapman, The Development of American Business and Banking Thought, 1913–1936 (1936) 用处较小。当时人的评估，见 Thomas Nixon Carver, The Present Economic Revolution in the United States (1925)；W. Z. Ripley, Main Street and Wall Street (1927)；J. T. Adams, Our Business Civilization (1929)；Adolf Berle and Gardiner Means, The Modern Corporation and Private Property (1932)；以及 Stuart Chase, Prosperity—Fact or Myth (1930)。Frederick Lewis Allen, The Lords of Creation (1935) 提供了一份可靠的二手记述。Sidney Ratner, American Taxation (1942) 是关于税收问题的最佳著作。

关于技术的主要资料有 U.S. National Resources Committee, Technological Trends and National Policy (1937)；Harry Jerome, Mechanization in Industry (1934)；Roger Burlingame, Engines of Democracy (1940)；Siegfried Giedion, Mechanization Takes Command (1948)；Walter Polakov, The Power Age (1933)；以及 Stuart Chase, Men and Machines (1929)。关于汽车，见 J. B. Rae 的 The Road and the Car in American Life (1971) 和 James J. Flink, The Car Culture (1975)。Allan Nevins and Frank Ernest Hill, Ford: Expansion and Challenge: 1915–1933 (1957) 是权威传记，但 Keith Sward, The Legend of Henry Ford (1948) 这部批判性极强的研究可资匡正。

期刊上对这些年份的分析，见 Joseph Schumpeter, “The American Economy in the Interwar Period: The Decade of the Twenties,” American Economic Review, XXXVI (1946)，附有 Garfield Cox 的评论；以及 Sumner Slichter, “The Period 1919–1936 in the United States: Its Significance for Business-Cycle Theory,” Review of Economic Statistics, XIX (1937)。其他有用的文章有 Norman Buchanan, “The Origin and Development of the Public Utility Holding Company,” Journal of Political Economy, XLIV (1936)；Sumner Slichter, “The Secret of High Wages,” New Republic, LIV (March 28, 1928)；“Swiss Family Dreyfus,” Fortune, VIII (September, 1933)；“A & P and the Hartfords,” Fortune, VII (1933)；“Woolworth,” Fortune, VIII (November, 1933)；N. R. Danielian, “From Insull to Injury,” Atlantic Monthly, CLI (1933)；以及 “Fifty Years: 1888–1938,” Printers’ Ink, CLXXXIV (July 28, 1938)。

John Higham, Strangers in the Land (1955) 是关于本土主义的一部杰出研究。关于限制移民运动的最佳简史，载于 Charles Howland (ed.), Survey of American Foreign Relations, 1929 (1929)。Kenneth Roberts, Why Europe Leaves Home (1922) 是一篇恶毒的论战文字。Robert DeC. Ward, “Our New Immigration Policy,” Foreign Affairs, III (September 15, 1924) 是为《民族来源法》所作的最有理据的辩护。Arthur Mann, “Gompers and the Irony of Racism,” Antioch Review, XIII (1953)，以及 William Bagley, “The Army Tests and the Pro-Nordic Propaganda,” Educational Review, LXVII (1924)，都是切题的文章。

Don S. Kirschner, City and Country (1970) 对中西部的城乡紧张关系极富启发，Charles W. Eagles, “Urban-Rural Conflict in the 1920’s: A Historiographic Assessment,” Historian, 49 (1986) 是一篇有用的综述。Eagles 的 Democracy Delayed (1990) 把围绕立法席位重新分配的争议置于城乡冲突的背景之中。Norman Furniss, The Fundamentalist Controversy, 1918–1931 (1954) 对这一题目的论述极为出色，应以 Ernest Sandeen, The Roots of Fundamentalism (1970)、George M. Marsden, Fundamentalism and American Culture (1980) 以及 H. Richard Niebuhr 为 Encyclopedia of the Social Sciences 撰写的简明词条 “Fundamentalism” 作为补充。Gail Kennedy (ed.), Evolution and Religion (1957) 和 E. C. Vanderlaan (ed.), Fundamentalism versus Modernism (1925) 是便于使用的资料汇编。William T. Doherty, “The Impact of Business on Protestantism, 1900–29,” Business History Review, XXVIII (1954) 是一篇饶有奇趣的记述。Lawrence W. Levine, William Jennings Bryan (1965) 和 Paolo E. Coletta, William Jennings Bryan (1969) 的第三卷，涵盖了这位“无双领袖”的最后十年。关于斯科普斯审判，见 Ray Ginger, Six Days or Forever? (1958)。Russell Owen, “The Significance of the Scopes Trial,” Current History, XXII (1925) 是当时最好的报道。

Andrew Sinclair, Prohibition: The Era of Excess (1962) 信息丰富，而且极为有趣。Joseph R. Gusfield, Symbolic Crusade (1963) 远远超出了其主题——禁酒运动——的范围，阐明了“表达性政治”的意义。Norman H. Clark, Deliver Us from Evil (1976) 和 David E. Kyvig, Repealing National Prohibition (1979) 都很重要。较早的评估，见 Charles Merz, The Dry Decade (1931)；Herbert Asbury, The Great Illusion (1950)；以及 Current History 1928年4月号。Virginius Dabney, Dry Messiah: The Life of Bishop Cannon (1949) 是一幅辛辣的肖像，对研究1928年竞选也有用处。

David M. Chalmers, Hooded Americanism (1965) 是一部清晰而视野广阔的三K党史。Kenneth T. Jackson, The Ku Klux Klan in the City (1967) 使我们这些曾把三K党主要视为乡村和小镇现象的人不得不修正自己的看法。Charles C. Alexander, The Ku Klux Klan in the Southwest (1965) 表明，逐个地区研究三K党是多么必要。其他地区性研究包括 Robert A. Goldberg, Hooded Empire (1981) 和 Leonard Moore, Citizen Klansmen (1991)，两者都把三K党视为一种民粹主义的表现，此外还有早得多的 Emerson Loucks, The Ku Klux Klan in Pennsylvania (1936)。Frank Tannenbaum, Darker Phases of the South (1924) 中有一章对三K党精神的起源作了敏锐的剖析。当时关于这一题目的最佳文章有 Morton Harrison, “Gentlemen from Indiana,” Atlantic Monthly, CXLI (1928)；R. A. Patton, “A Ku Klux Klan Reign of Terror,” Current History, XXVIII (1928)；以及 Frank Bohn, “The Ku Klux Klan Interpreted,” American Journal of Sociology, XXX (1925)。Robert Moats Miller, “A Note on the Relationship between the Protestant Churches and the Revived Ku Klux Klan,” Journal of Social History, XXII (1956) 颇有助益。

David Burner, The Politics of Provincialism (1968) 是关于“转型中的民主党”的一部重要研究，J. Joseph Huthmacher, Massachusetts People and Politics, 1919–1933 (1959) 对族裔因素对投票的影响论述精彩。Oscar Handlin, Al Smith and His America (1958) 论述了这位民主党候选人，Allan Lichtman, Prejudice and the Old Politics (1978) 则强调宗教偏见在1928年的影响。Elisabeth Israels Perry, Belle Moskowitz (1987) 对史密斯最重要的顾问作了富有启发的论述。

Samuel Lubell, The Future of American Politics (1952) 以生动的笔调论证了“城市的反叛”对1928年竞选的重要意义，但把论点推得过远。Lubell 大量依赖 Samuel Eldersveld, “Influence of Metropolitan Party Pluralities in Presidential Elections since 1920,” American Political Science Review, XLIII (1949)，而该文受到 Jerome Clubb and Howard Allen, “The Cities and the Election of 1928,” American Historical Review, LXXIV (1969) 的有力质疑。Roy Peel and Thomas Donnelly, The 1928 Campaign: An Analysis (1931) 是一部可靠的简要记述。Edmund Moore, A Catholic Runs for President (1956) 对宗教问题的论述极为出色。V. O. Key, Jr., Southern Politics (1949) 有重要的一章，把1928年的“胡佛民主党人”与1948年的“迪克西党人”作了比较。Frank Freidel, Franklin D. Roosevelt: The Ordeal (1954) 是研究20世纪20年代民主党历史的宝贵资料。史密斯最可畏的对手的言论，见 James Cannon, Jr., “Al Smith—Catholic, Tammany, Wet,” Nation, CXXVII (July 4, 1928)，以及 “Causes of Governor Smith’s Defeat,” Current History, XXIX (1928)。Washington Pezet, “The Temporal Power of Evangelism,” Forum, LXXVI (1926) 和 Robert Moats Miller, “A Footnote to the Role of the Protestant Churches in the Election of 1928,” Church History, XXV (1956)，对新教的政治影响持不同看法。

上文所列关于经济的大多数著作也适用于股市崩盘和大萧条。Robert Sobel, The Great Bull Market (1968) 记述了华尔街的全盛时期。John Brooks, Once in Golconda (1969) 文笔优雅。Milton Friedman and Anna Schwartz, The Great Contraction, 1929–1933 (1965) 应与 Peter Temin, Did Monetary Forces Cause the Great Depression? (1976) 对照阅读。专题研究包括 Lionel Robbins, The Great Depression (1934) 和 Francis Hirst, Wall Street and Lombard Street (1931)。Charles Merz, “Bull Market,” Harper’s, CLVII (1929) 生动地描述了投机狂热。Joe Alex Morris, What a Year! (1956) 对1929年作了一番浮光掠影式的扫描。John Kenneth Galbraith, The Great Crash (1955) 以绝妙的冷幽默写成。Broadus Mitchell, Depression Decade (1947) 是最佳的经济史。E. Jay Howenstine, Jr., “World War I Production Dislocations as a Causal Factor of the Great Depression in the United States,” American Journal of Economics and Sociology, XIII (1954) 淡化了第一次世界大战的重要性。Gilbert Seldes, Years of the Locust (1933)、Mauritz Hallgren, Seeds of Revolt (1933) 和 Jonathan Leonard, Three Years Down (1939) 是关于大萧条的早期社会史。

关于赫伯特·胡佛的最新研究有 Martin L. Fausold and George T. Mazuzan, eds., The Hoover Presidency (1974)；Joan Hoff Wilson, Herbert Hoover (1975)；David Burner, Herbert Hoover (1978)；George Nash, The Life of Herbert Hoover (1983)，这是一部计划中的多卷本传记的第一卷；以及 Martin L. Fausold, The Presidency of Herbert Hoover (1985)。Albert U. Romasco, The Poverty of Abundance (1965) 和 Jordan A. Schwarz, The Interregnum of Despair (1970) 对胡佛政策的缺陷阐述得尤为透彻。其他重要专著有 James S. Olson, Herbert Hoover and the Reconstruction Finance Corporation, 1931–1933 (1977)；William J. Barber, From New Era to New Deal (1985)；以及 David E. Hamilton, From New Day to New Deal (1991)。为胡佛辩护的论述见于他的 Memoirs (3 vols., 1951–52)、W. S. Myers and W. H. Newton, The Hoover Administration (1936) 以及 Ray Lyman Wilbur and Arthur Hyde, The Hoover Policies (1937)。胡佛在 “Boyhood in Iowa,” Palimpsest, IX (1928) 和 “In Praise of Izaak Walton,” Atlantic Monthly, CXXXIX (1927) 中强调了自己的乡村出身。T. G. Joslin, Hoover—Off the Record (1934) 是热情的辩护；R. G. Tugwell, Mr. Hoover’s Economic Policy (1932) 持批评态度。Walter Lippmann, “The Peculiar Weakness of Mr. Hoover,” Harper’s, CLXI (1930)；Allan Nevins, “President Hoover’s Record,” Current History, XXXVI (1932)；William Allen White, “Herbert Hoover—the Last of the Old Presidents or the First of the New,” Saturday Evening Post, CCV (March 4, 1933)；以及 Mark Sullivan, “The Case for the Administration,” Fortune, VI (1932)，提供了相互对立的看法。关于胡佛任期最后一年里最富戏剧性的事件，见 Roger Daniels, The Bonus March (1971) 和 Donald Lisio, The President and Protest (1974)。

关于大萧条造成的苦难，两篇最佳记述是 “‘No One Has Starved,’” Fortune, VI (1932) 和 “New York in Third Winter,” Fortune, V (1932)。John Maynard Keynes, “The World’s Economic Outlook,” Atlantic Monthly, CXLIX (1932)；Elmer Davis, “Confidence in Whom?” Forum, LXXXIX (1933)；Paul Sifton, “Going, Going, Gone!” Forum, LXXXIX (1933)；J. Russell Smith, “The End of an Epoch,” Survey, LXVI (July 1, 1931)；James Truslow Adams, “Shadow of the Man on Horseback,” Atlantic Monthly, CXLIX (1932)；以及 Reinhold Niebuhr, “Catastrophe or Social Control?” Harper’s, CLXV (1932)，无不忧心忡忡。David Salmon, Confessions of a Former Customers’ Man (1932) 是股市崩盘催生的那类忏悔文学的典型。该书开篇写道：“近15年来，我一直是一个金融寄生虫和掮客……”R. V. Peel and T. C. Donnelly, The 1932 Campaign (1935)、Rexford Tugwell, The Democratic Roosevelt (1957)，尤其是 Frank Freidel, Franklin D. Roosevelt: The Triumph (1956)，对1932年总统选战作了很好的描述。

# 致谢

在本书初版的末尾，我曾向丹尼尔·阿伦（Daniel Aaron）、理查德·霍夫施塔特、琼·麦金泰尔·洛伊希滕堡（Jean McIntire Leuchtenburg）和阿瑟·林克（Arthur Link）致以谢意，他们通读了全部书稿，提出了极为宝贵的建议；我也感谢哥伦比亚大学图书馆的工作人员，以及厄尔敏·斯通（Ermine Stone）和萨拉·劳伦斯学院图书馆的工作人员，他们给予的关照远远超出了对一位邻人应尽的待客之道。我当时还曾提到，本书的许多内容最初是在我于哥伦比亚大学主持的研究生研讨课上与学生们反复讨论而成的。

至于这个修订版，我最应感谢的无疑是琼·安妮·洛伊希滕堡（Jean Anne Leuchtenburg）。她在文字处理机前度过了无数个日子，以敏锐的眼光审读了新的书稿，而且自始至终都带着她一贯的优雅与好兴致。
